    SELECT "u"."membership_id", "u"."assigned_at", "u"."assigned_by", "u"."group_id", "u"."user_id", "a"."user_id" AS "user_id0", "a"."created_at", "a"."created_by", "a"."display_name", "a"."email", "a"."is_active", "a"."password_algorithm", "a"."password_hash", "a"."username"
    FROM "user_group_memberships" AS "u"
    INNER JOIN "app_users" AS "a" ON "u"."user_id" = "a"."user_id"
) AS "s" ON "p"."group_id" = "s"."group_id"
ORDER BY "p"."group_name", "p"."group_id", "g"."permission_id", "s"."membership_id"

FROM "app_users" AS "a"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "u"."membership_id", "u"."assigned_at", "u"."assigned_by", "u"."group_id", "u"."user_id", "p"."group_id" AS "group_id0", "p"."created_at", "p"."description", "p"."group_name", "p"."is_active", "p"."is_system"
